OR1-orðalag staðfest beint í gögnum (ekki aðeins í prófum): Akranes "2,5"/"21441"+óleyst, Dalvík "3501"/"innri", Kirkjubæjarklaustur "2409"+óleyst, Höfn "982"/"3170"+EKKI-afneitun, Kópavogur "gegnumveg" án "gatnamót" hvar sem er, Hafnarfjörður nefnir afturkallaðan 2807 START og endurvekur hann ekki. api/_lib/winterRoute/approachSelections.test.js framlengt (nýr óháður EXPECTED_PRODUCT/-NATIVE listi handskrifaður úr prompt-töflu, ekki candidate-review.json; literal-equality á hrágögnum; OR2 Math.abs<1e-7; OR3 nærverupróf; 6 OR1-orðalagspróf; raunveruleg mutation/negative-próf) + 5 önnur prófasöfn (places/remainingCoverage/acquiredGeometry/approachAnalysis/placeEvidence) með réttu 25-27-undirmengi í sínum eigin SELECTED_PRODUCT_APPROACH_IDS -- southConnections þarf ekkert (engin skörun). ÁRÍÐANDI: npx vitest run er óvirkt í þessu umhverfi fyrir ALLAR prófaskrár, líka ósnertar (staðfest á routes.test.js, sama villa, reyndi cache-hreinsun + --pool=forks, gafst upp eftir 2 tilraunir samkvæmt reglu um hjálpartækjabil) -- líklega Node24.11.0 vs CI-Node20 (CLAUDE.md), ekki breytt hér (utan umfangs). Þess í stað: ESLint á 6 snertum prófskrám hreint, git diff --check hreint, git status aðeins leyfðar skrár, og eigin 145-atriða beinn Python-samanburður á lifandi gögnum (144/144 efnisleg atriði PASS, 1 falsk-jákvæð villa í eigin regex-athugun ótengd þessari lotu). Prófakóði ÓKEYRÐUR þessa lotu -- skrái það hreinskilnislega sem opið atriði, ekki PASS-fullyrðing. Engin app/UI/leiðarvél, engin net/GitHub/commit/push/ný dependency. #500 áfram opið: 54/56 með punkt, 2 án (Keflavík bær, Þórshöfn -- engin retained source-geometry, engin ný öflun hér), 0 SUPPORTED, EXACT_GAP á öllum. Næst: Jonesy endurkeyrir vitest í virku umhverfi áður en RESULT_REVIEW treystir á prófatölu úr þessari lotu.

Wrote rules-addendum-v3.md FIRST (Z1), replacing the walker with a directed-instance graph + Dijkstra shortest path where every VEGHLUTI 3/4/6/7 piece has exactly ONE traversable direction as a hard graph-construction constraint. Caught and fixed two real bugs in my own first v3 draft before reporting: (1) VEGHLUTI=8 (piece 1735, the single nationwide instance) was first bucketed with the one-directional roles, silently making the real long route unreachable (Dijkstra returned the short route in disguise, labeled with the wrong length -- caught because the number didn't match expectations, not assumed correct) -- fixed by restoring rules.md's own original E2(b) treatment of role 8 as its own distinct UNKNOWN/bidirectional case; (2) the long-route target was initially (3876,"fwd") when C is actually 3876's own START, i.e. the "rev" instance -- fixed and confirmed against akureyri-endpoint-decision.md's own native C coordinate; (3) the long-route start point initially let the search silently bypass piece 1436 entirely via a zero-gap shortcut -- fixed to start from 1436's own END (the same anchor the short route uses, per #491's own convention), forcing 1436 to be genuinely traversed as the route's own first step, matching #483's reference which explicitly counts it. Positive reference tests now pass via genuine search, never hardcoded: counterclockwise Dijkstra reproduces the EXACT 29-IDFERILL #491 order; clockwise Dijkstra reproduces the EXACT 13-IDBUTUR #483 order and reaches the exact C coordinate; zero negative-invariant violations either way, asserted over the full sequence. Long-route lengths (925.8028/928.3630km) independently cross-validate v1/v2's own greedy-walker figures to 4 decimal places -- two different algorithms, same canonical data, same answer. Short route's canonical-basis figure (70.8466km) differs from #491's own JSON-stated 70.78km by 0.0666km, explained (same gaps, different per-piece vertex digitization), not just noted. Bridge/seam counts now computed from the actual path, not hand-typed: Hella 4 (highest 42.30m), long-to-C 6 (highest 42.30m), short 16 to Borgarnes alone (highest 41.45m, matching #491 exactly) / 18 including the Borgarnes roundabout's own two measured seams (highest 42.84m, matching north-data exactly). Closure test redefined at the route level (Z4): CLOSED if the fixed, already-computed shortest path's own piece list contains an IDBUTUR in the closure set -- a membership check, not a graph search (which can never disconnect a cycle); non-vacuity confirmed with an unrelated closure set. Ring total (1308.7937km) relabeled as a sum of two separately-sourced legs, not a verified closure -- the clockwise Borgarnes->Rauðavatn leg is NOT_RUN this round; the "other way around" estimate corrected to ≈1263.05km on a consistent basis. Fixed AKUREYRI's inventory node (now the actual end of piece 2940 = C), ANCHOR (both ends of 1436 recorded, length-origin end named), and MOSFELLSBAER (reverted to NODE_UNDETERMINED, its v2 node was a name mismatch). 2026-11-04 (CC): Executed approved-prompt-v4.md (correction round) for #491. V1: h1/Helmet `<title>` now derive from the selected `WINTER_ROUTE_OPTIONS` entry's `nameIs`/`nameEn` (memoized `selectedRouteOption`/`routeName`), computed every render — follows the `<select>` immediately, including while the new route's data is loading or has failed; the now-unused `winterRouteTitle` translation key was removed (nothing else referenced it). Side effect: Vík's h1 text changes from the key's un-spaced en dash to the options table's spaced one, matching the `<select>`'s own option text — intentional, per the prompt's own "leiða af valinni WINTER_ROUTE_OPTIONS færslu" wording; two pre-existing `ticket-486.spec.js` exact-text assertions updated to match. V2: the real bug was that `setData(null)`/`setLoadError(null)` ran inside the `routeId` *passive* effect, which commits one render pass after the `routeId` state change itself — not "the same render pass" as the v3 report/code comment wrongly claimed. Fix: the effect no longer clears either state; instead `view`/`viewErrorKey` are derived at render time (`data?.routeId === routeId ? data : null`, and `loadError` now stored as `{ routeId, key }` masked the same way), so the masking lands in the exact same commit as the `routeId` change. All render-path consumers (`RouteSummary`, `RouteComparison`, forecast/road sections, `availableHours`) now read `view`/`viewErrorKey`. Added a React-`Profiler`-based frame-level regression test (3 cases: stale data, stale error, late-arriving old-route response) that inspects every commit, not just post-`act` state — this is what actually proves no bad frame occurs, which the old test couldn't see. The code comment and report both now carry an explicit correction section (history kept, not deleted) rather than silently rewriting the earlier wrong claim. V3: new `docs/ai/tasks/ticket-491/derivation-29-pieces.md` with source files+SHA256, method, the full 29-row table, and the 13-row Vík control, plus one inline offline Python command to reproduce both. **Finding, not fixed (per the prompt's own fallback — documented and stopped, not expanded):** the 13-Vík control fully reconciles (max 0.0039 km, matching the already-reported figure) once two real re-derivation bugs were found and corrected (IDFERILL reused across 4 precision tiers per feature — must filter `NAKVAEMNIFERLIS==0` before indexing; Vík's multi-piece IDBUTUR groups can include an unrelated branch road, distinguishable by a non-lettered KAFLANR) — but the 29-piece Borgarnes chain's P1/P3/P4 do NOT reconcile to the ±0.01km tolerance against `routes.js`'s fixed figures (each ≈0.037 km low; P2 is right at the boundary, 0.0101 km over). Ruled out: the same tier bug, alternate native-gap endpoint-pairing strategies, an obvious unrelated-branch piece inside the 29-piece list. This supersedes the v3 report's "0.000 km deviation on all four points" claim (that script was disposable/never retained, so could not be re-checked). `routes.js`'s numbers were NOT changed. R4: no action. R5: wording-only correction (handler always passes an explicit routeId; "implicit" described a direct-caller/test path only). Validation: `WinterRoute.test.jsx` 55/55 (7 new tests); full vitest 207 files/3093 tests passing; lint clean; build succeeds; Playwright real-Chromium 19/19 (fixed 3 assertions made ambiguous by the h1/option text now matching, via `getByRole("heading", ...)`; added+then removed a `toHaveTitle` assertion after finding `document.title` stays empty in this harness regardless of route — a pre-existing, unrelated react-helmet-async/dev-server issue, reproducible even on the untouched default Vík load, logged as an unresolved UNKNOWN, not chased). No backend/cache/route-data/ETA/provider/DB/payment/config change; no new network/key/dependency/subagent; no commit/push/GitHub/deploy. CC_COMPLETE. Production NOT_TESTED. Next: owner → Jonesy ("CC búinn") → Ripley final assessment — including a decision on the V3 P1/P3/P4 reproducibility gap and the document-title UNKNOWN.
